### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP は保証せず速さを優先する**点です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tus commits locales al día con la rama principal y dejar el historial lineal, siempre que esa rama no la haya usado nadie más. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o la comparten otras personas, porque reescribir su historia les causaría conflictos.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接、按字节流传输的，保证数据不丢、不乱、按序到达；UDP 是不可靠的、无连接的，只管尽快把数据包发出去，不保证送达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and you want a clean, linear history: updating your feature branch onto the latest main, or tidying WIP commits before review. Merge when others already have the branch or you want the real history kept, like merging a shared or long-lived branch into main.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

如果只记得文件名的一部分，也可以只写关键字，Bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件：

```bash
bun test parse
```

要连文件里的某一个用例都不跑，用 `-t` 按用例名过滤：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts -t "空字符串"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database writes down every change before it applies that change to the main data. If the system crashes midway, it can replay the log on restart to recover the changes that were committed but not yet fully saved.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着改那个 compaction 的 PR 的锁粒度，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说：「接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点」

要做的：
- 按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把锁的范围缩小，只锁真正需要保护的部分。
- 保持现有测试通过；如果改动影响并发行为，补一个并发相关的测试。
- 改动只在这个 PR 的分支上提交，不合并、不推送到主分支、不改 PR 的状态（仍是 draft）。

完成标准：
- 说明改了哪些锁、锁的范围前后怎么变。
- 报告测试的运行结果（实际跑过的命令和输出），不能只凭自己说。

不在范围内：
- 不做其他重构，不改 compaction 的逻辑本身。
- 不标记 PR 为 ready，不发任何评论。
```

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1873 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<script>
function render(d) {
  var nights = (d && d.nights) || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!nights.length) {
    var e = document.createElement('div');
    e.className = 'k-empty';
    e.textContent = 'Each night’s result will list here.';
    list.appendChild(e);
    return;
  }
  var latest = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = latest.rate + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = latest.label + ' · p99 ' + latest.p99 + ' · compaction stalls: ' + latest.stalls;
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
    row.querySelector('.k-row-main').textContent = n.label;
    row.querySelector('.k-row-sub') || null;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · compaction stalls: ' + n.stalls;
    row.querySelector('.k-row-main').appendChild(sub);
    row.querySelector('.k-row-value').textContent = n.rate + ' /s';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
var v = ducter.get('nights');
if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') { v.then(render); } else { render({ nights: v }); }
ducter.onChange(function () {
  var w = ducter.get('nights');
  if (w && typeof w.then === 'function') { w.then(function (x) { render({ nights: x }); }); } else { render({ nights: w }); }
});
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"label":"Night 2","rate":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"label":"Night 3","rate":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

    var t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
    var ml = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if (!/^([01]?\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(t)) return;
    if (!(ml > 0)) return;
    feeds.push({ d: today(), t: t.length === 4 ? '0' + t : t, ml: ml });
    save();
    document.getElementById('t').value = '';
    document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
    document.querySelector('details.k-add').open = false;
    render();
  });

  function load(v) {
    feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(load);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(load);
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>

  .k-row { position: relative; padding-left: 36px; }
  .k-row label { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; cursor: pointer; width: 100%; }
  .k-row label input[type=checkbox] { position: absolute; opacity: 0; pointer-events: none; }
  .k-row::before {
    content: ""; position: absolute; left: 4px; top: 14px; width: 18px; height: 18px;
    border: 2px solid var(--d-line, #ccc); border-radius: 5px; background: var(--d-card, #fff);
  }
  .k-row:has(input:checked)::before { background: var(--d-good, #2a9d62); border-color: var(--d-good, #2a9d62); }
  .k-row:has(input:checked) .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted, #888); }
</style>

<script>
  var KEY = 'landed';
  var ITEMS = 'items';
  var list = document.getElementById('list');

  function draw(items) {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    (items || … endChild(label);
      list.appendChild(li);
      box.addEventListener('change', save);
    });
  }

  function boxes() { return list.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]'); }

  function update() {
    var all = boxes(), left = 0;
    all.forEach(function (b) { if (!b.checked) left++; });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = left;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = left === 0
      ? 'All ' + all.length + ' landed.'
      : 'of ' + all.length + ' PRs. Tick each one off as it lands.';
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(left === 0 ? 'All PRs landed' : left + ' PRs waiting');
    }
  }

  function apply(landed) {
    var s = landed || {};
    boxes().forEach(function (b) { b.checked = !!s[b.dataset.id]; });
    update();
  }

  function save() {
    var s = {};
    boxes().forEach(function (b) { if (b.checked) s[b.dataset.id] = true; });
    ducter.set(KEY, s);
    update();
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(ITEMS)).then(function (items) {
    draw(items);
    return Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY));
  }).then(apply);

  ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(apply); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","title":"compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","from":"你的 PR 列表 (PRs waiting on someone)"},{"id":"mem-10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","title":"retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing","from":"你的 PR 列表 (PRs waiting on someone)"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","title":"flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","from":"你的 PR 列表 (PRs waiting on someone)"},{"id":"ducter-418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","title":"apps bar","sub":"Draft, not waiting on anyone yet","from":"你的 PR 列表 (PRs waiting on someone)"}]}

<div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>

</details>
</main>
<script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat="交通", edit=-1;
const money=n=>"¥"+Number(n||0).toLocaleString("en-US",{maximumFractionDigits:2});
const esc=s=>String(s==null?"":s).repla … xtContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记一笔就会在这里合计";
  $("list").innerHTML=xs.length?xs.map((x,i)=>'<div class="k-row" data-i="'+i+'"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+' · '+esc(x.cat||"")+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.note||"")+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+esc(money(x.amt))+'</div></div>').join(""):'<div class="k-empty">暂无花销，记一笔就会列在这里</div>';
  document.querySelectorAll("#list .k-row").forEach(r=>r.onclick=()=>{const x=xs[+r.dataset.i];edit=+r.dataset.i;$("trip").value=x.trip||"";$("amt").value=x.amt||"";$("note").value=x.note||"";pick(x.cat||"交通");$("save").textContent="改好了";$("del").hidden=false;$("addbox").open=true});
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(total):"还没记");
}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);

};
$("del").onclick=()=>{const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];xs.splice(edit,1);ducter.set("items",xs);clearForm();draw()};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个 3D 风格的卡通笔记本电脑：奶油色机身，深蓝色屏幕边框，屏幕是空白的，键盘和触控板也画得很清楚。整体是柔和的黏土质感。

适合放在空状态里。屏幕空白正好表达“这里还没有内容”，画面又温和友好，不会让人觉得是出错了。

有几点要注意：

- 它是通用的笔记本图，没有和具体场景相关的线索。如果空状态是“还没有文档”或“还没有笔记”，可以在屏幕上加个小元素，或者配一句说明文字。
- 320×246 偏小，放大后可能会糊，最好找原始的高清版本。
- 奶油色在浅色背景上对比度不高，如果页面背景也是浅色，图会有点淡。可以试试放在浅灰或者米色的卡片里。
- 如果你的界面整体风格偏冷、偏简洁，这套暖色的 3D 插画会显得有点突出。要统一风格的话，最好整套插画用同一种质感。
```
